					ROOT = { remove_from_faction = PREV }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_country = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT is_literally_china = yes } }

					min = 0

					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_fascism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@fascism }

					var = ROOT_fascism_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}

						has_government = democratic
					}

						has_government = neutrality
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
				}

					}

				}

					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
				}

					}

						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}

						neutrality = ROOT_neutrality_popularity

						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag

			else_if = {
				limit = { has_idea = warlord_state3 }
				add_political_power = 45
				add_stability = 0.04
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = warlord_state3
					add_idea = CHI_economy_in_the_gutter
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_idea = warlord_state4 }
				add_political_power = 75
				add_stability = 0.05
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = warlord_state4
					add_idea = CHI_economy_in_the_gutter
				}
			}
			else = { add_ideas = CHI_economy_in_the_gutter }
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = CHI_kmt_economic_influence }
				remove_ideas = CHI_kmt_economic_influence
				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			}
			CHI = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = CHI_opposition target = ROOT } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject_of = CHI }
				OVERLORD = {
					set_autonomy = { target = ROOT autonomy_state = autonomy_free }
					create_wargoal = {
						target = ROOT
						type = puppet_wargoal_focus
						expire = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				every_country = {
					limit = { is_literally_china = yes }
					ROOT = { remove_from_faction = PREV }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_country = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT is_literally_china = yes } }				
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction_with = ROOT
						is_faction_leader = yes
					}
					remove_from_faction = ROOT
				}
			}		
		}
	}
